--
-- TOC entry 231 (class 1255 OID 17226)
-- Name: basket_quality_score(integer); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: public; Owner: postgres
--

CREATE FUNCTION public.basket_quality_score(p_foodbasket_id integer) RETURNS numeric
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$
DECLARE
  v_score numeric := 10;

  r record;
  v_cal numeric;
  v_pro numeric;
  v_fat numeric;
  v_carb numeric;

--טוען את כל המוצרים שבסל
  cur CURSOR FOR
    SELECT g.groceries_id, g.type
    FROM foodbasket_groceries fg
    JOIN groceries g ON g.groceries_id = fg.groceries_id
    WHERE fg.foodbasket_id = p_foodbasket_id;
BEGIN
  -- סל חייב להתקיים
  IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM foodbasket WHERE foodbasket_id = p_foodbasket_id) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'foodbasket_id % not found', p_foodbasket_id;
  END IF;

  OPEN cur;
  LOOP
    FETCH cur INTO r;
    EXIT WHEN NOT FOUND;

    -- להביא ערכים תזונתיים של המוצר (אם חסר ערך -> יהיה NULL)
    SELECT
      MAX(CASE WHEN nutrition_id = 1 THEN value END), -- Calories
      MAX(CASE WHEN nutrition_id = 2 THEN value END), -- Protein
      MAX(CASE WHEN nutrition_id = 3 THEN value END), -- Fat
      MAX(CASE WHEN nutrition_id = 4 THEN value END)  -- Carbs
    INTO v_cal, v_pro, v_fat, v_carb
    FROM groceries_nutrition
    WHERE groceries_id = r.groceries_id;

    -- type = 'B' (בסיסי)
    IF r.type = 'B' THEN
      IF v_fat IS NOT NULL AND v_fat > 150 THEN v_score := v_score - 1; END IF;
      IF v_cal IS NOT NULL AND v_cal > 25  THEN v_score := v_score - 1; END IF;
      IF v_pro IS NOT NULL AND v_pro < 100 THEN v_score := v_score - 1; END IF;
      IF v_carb IS NOT NULL AND v_carb > 30 THEN v_score := v_score - 1; END IF;

    -- type = 'M' (ארוחה)
    ELSIF r.type = 'M' THEN
      IF v_pro  IS NOT NULL AND v_pro  < 30 THEN v_score := v_score - 1; END IF;
      IF v_cal  IS NOT NULL AND v_cal  > 50 THEN v_score := v_score - 1; END IF;
      IF v_fat  IS NOT NULL AND v_fat  > 70 THEN v_score := v_score - 1; END IF;
      IF v_carb IS NOT NULL AND v_carb < 30 THEN v_score := v_score - 1; END IF;
    END IF;

  END LOOP;
  CLOSE cur;

-- גבולות: 0..10
  IF v_score < 0 THEN v_score := 0; END IF;
  IF v_score > 10 THEN v_score := 10; END IF;
  
  RETURN v_score;
END;
$$;


ALTER FUNCTION public.basket_quality_score(p_foodbasket_id integer) OWNER TO postgres;

--
-- TOC entry 246 (class 1255 OID 17201)
-- Name: calculate_courier_bonus(integer); Type: PROCEDURE; Schema: public; Owner: postgres
--

CREATE PROCEDURE public.calculate_courier_bonus(IN p_courier_id integer)
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$
DECLARE
	p_salary TEXT;
	p_foodbasket_count INT;
	p_bonus NUMERIC(10,2);
	
BEGIN
	SELECT salary
	INTO p_salary
	FROM courier 
	WHERE courier_id = p_courier_id; 

	-- בדיקה האם העובד קיים במאגר
	IF NOT FOUND THEN
			RAISE EXCEPTION 'courier % dosenwt exist', p_courier_id;
	END IF;

	-- בדיקה האם עובד מקבל משכורת או לא
	IF p_salary <> 'N' THEN 
		RAISE NOTICE 'courier % received salary- no bonuse', p_courier_id;
	-- בדיקה כמה סלים המתנדב חילק
	ELSE
		SELECT COUNT(*)
		INTO p_foodbasket_count
		FROM foodbasket
		WHERE courier_id = p_courier_id AND dateofrecepit <= CURRENT_DATE;

		--חישוב מענק
		p_bonus := p_foodbasket_count * 10;

		--עדכון המענק בטבלת העובדים
		UPDATE courier 
		SET bonus = p_bonus
		WHERE courier_id = p_courier_id;

		RAISE NOTICE 'courier % received bonuse', p_courier_id;
		
	END IF;
-- טיפול בשגיאות
EXCEPTION
	WHEN OTHERS THEN
		ROLLBACK;
		RAISE NOTICE 'ERROR: %', SQLERRM;

END;
$$;


ALTER PROCEDURE public.calculate_courier_bonus(IN p_courier_id integer) OWNER TO postgres;

--
-- TOC entry 247 (class 1255 OID 17227)
-- Name: fix_courier_city_mismatch(); Type: PROCEDURE; Schema: public; Owner: postgres
--

CREATE PROCEDURE public.fix_courier_city_mismatch()
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$
DECLARE
  r record;
  v_new_courier_id int;

  cur CURSOR FOR
    SELECT
      b.foodbasket_id,
      b.courier_id,
      f.city_id AS family_city_id,
      c.city_id AS courier_city_id
    FROM foodbasket b
    JOIN family f  ON f.family_id = b.family_id
    JOIN courier c ON c.courier_id = b.courier_id
    WHERE b.family_id IS NOT NULL
      AND b.courier_id IS NOT NULL
      AND b.status <> 'delivered';
BEGIN
  OPEN cur;

  LOOP
    FETCH cur INTO r;
    EXIT WHEN NOT FOUND;

    BEGIN
      -- אם השליח כבר תואם לעיר המשפחה – ממשיכים
      IF r.family_city_id = r.courier_city_id THEN
        CONTINUE;
      END IF;

      -- חיפוש שליח בעיר של המשפחה
      SELECT courier_id
      INTO v_new_courier_id
      FROM courier
      WHERE city_id = r.family_city_id
      ORDER BY courier_id
      LIMIT 1;

      -- אם לא נמצא שליח מתאים – הדפסה וממשיכים
      IF v_new_courier_id IS NULL THEN
        RAISE NOTICE 'No suitable courier found for basket %', r.foodbasket_id;
      ELSE
        -- נמצא שליח מתאים – מעדכנים
        UPDATE foodbasket
        SET courier_id = v_new_courier_id
        WHERE foodbasket_id = r.foodbasket_id;
      END IF;

    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
      RAISE NOTICE 'For basket %: skipped due to error: %',
        r.foodbasket_id, SQLERRM;
    END;
  END LOOP;

  CLOSE cur;
END;
$$;


ALTER PROCEDURE public.fix_courier_city_mismatch() OWNER TO postgres;

--
-- TOC entry 249 (class 1255 OID 17229)
-- Name: log_foodbasket_status_change(); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: public; Owner: postgres
--

CREATE FUNCTION public.log_foodbasket_status_change() RETURNS trigger
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$
BEGIN
    -- נרשם רק אם הסטטוס באמת השתנה
    IF OLD.status IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.status THEN
        INSERT INTO foodbasket_status_log
        (foodbasket_id, old_status, new_status)
        VALUES
        (OLD.foodbasket_id, OLD.status, NEW.status);
    END IF;

    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;


ALTER FUNCTION public.log_foodbasket_status_change() OWNER TO postgres;

--
-- TOC entry 248 (class 1255 OID 17228)
-- Name: main2(); Type: PROCEDURE; Schema: public; Owner: postgres
--

CREATE PROCEDURE public.main2()
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$
DECLARE
  r record;
  v_score numeric;
BEGIN
  -- זימון הפרוצדורה
  CALL fix_courier_city_mismatch();

  -- זימון הפונקציה עבור כל סל
  FOR r IN
    SELECT foodbasket_id
    FROM foodbasket
	ORDER BY foodbasket_id ASC
  LOOP
    v_score := basket_quality_score(r.foodbasket_id);

    -- הדפסה בלבד
    RAISE NOTICE 'For basket %: quality score = %',
      r.foodbasket_id, v_score;
  END LOOP;
END;
$$;


ALTER PROCEDURE public.main2() OWNER TO postgres;

--
-- TOC entry 243 (class 1255 OID 17206)
-- Name: rank_couriers_by_baskets(); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: public; Owner: postgres
--

CREATE FUNCTION public.rank_couriers_by_baskets() RETURNS refcursor
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$
DECLARE
    ref REFCURSOR;
BEGIN
    OPEN ref FOR
        SELECT 
			c.courier_id,
			c.courier_name,
			count (f.foodbasket_id) AS foodbasket_count
		FROM courier c
		LEFT JOIN foodbasket f
			  ON c.courier_id = f.courier_id AND f.dateofrecepit <= CURRENT_DATE
		GROUP BY c.courier_id, c.courier_name
		ORDER BY foodbasket_count DESC;
   
   RETURN ref;
END;
$$;


ALTER FUNCTION public.rank_couriers_by_baskets() OWNER TO postgres;

--
-- TOC entry 245 (class 1255 OID 17217)
-- Name: rank_couriers_by_baskets_update(); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: public; Owner: postgres
--

CREATE FUNCTION public.rank_couriers_by_baskets_update() RETURNS void
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$
BEGIN
    -- מחיקת כל הרשומות הישנות בטבלת דירוג
    DELETE FROM courier_ranking;

    -- הוספת הדירוג החדש לטבלה
    INSERT INTO courier_ranking(courier_id, courier_name, foodbasket_count)
    SELECT 
        c.courier_id,
        c.courier_name,
        COUNT(f.foodbasket_id) AS foodbasket_count
    FROM courier c
    LEFT JOIN foodbasket f
        ON c.courier_id = f.courier_id 
       AND f.dateofrecepit <= CURRENT_DATE
    GROUP BY c.courier_id, c.courier_name
    ORDER BY foodbasket_count DESC;

END;
$$;


ALTER FUNCTION public.rank_couriers_by_baskets_update() OWNER TO postgres;

--
-- TOC entry 244 (class 1255 OID 17215)
-- Name: update_courier_ranking(); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: public; Owner: postgres
--

CREATE FUNCTION public.update_courier_ranking() RETURNS trigger
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$

BEGIN
	-- מחיקת הרשומות הישנות
	DELETE FROM courier_ranking WHERE courier_id = NEW.courier_id;

	-- הוספת הדירוג המעודכן
	INSERT INTO courier_ranking(courier_id, courier_name, foodbasket_count)
	SELECT 
		c.courier_id,
		c.courier_name,
		count (f.foodbasket_id) 
	FROM courier c
	LEFT JOIN foodbasket f
		  ON c.courier_id = f.courier_id 
	WHERE c.courier_id = NEW.courier_id
	GROUP BY c.courier_id, c.courier_name;
   
   RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;


ALTER FUNCTION public.update_courier_ranking() OWNER TO postgres;
